# Seed 41 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - small: the JIT genes spread, the gene set converging (Iteration 198)

`seed 41 tag2 6h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier
--cpus 2,8`, commit b774e142 (the after-seed-40 batch), started 14:45: the
fronts of seeds 37-40 carried in, population 256, four workers; the run packed
at 18:50, the comparison (session 36) at 20:54. 18,213 designs, 17,306 alive.

Session 36 against seeds 37-40, by the five measures (results/gp-deep-dive.md):
fastest +1.6% (9e2a6f1307 0.183 against seed 40's d9247d19b0 0.186), smallest
+0.00% (f0aeaeb237, 17,938 bytes), memory -4 KB, hypervolume +1.2%, best Q -0.2%:
small. The run's own fastest, 28677d13b1 at 0.174, measured 0.185 in the
session - the winner's curse of 18,000 noisy measurements, which the
comparison exists to catch. The Q-best of all runs stays seed 40's 528c2523c3.

Deaths: 858 reach limit at scale 0 (not run), 37 short branch out of range
(-131 to -135: the converter sizes branches before the tag-2 map - all tag-2
with hotcalls, 34 with escape and rtloop; seed 40 had none), 12 corpus (every
one opcall 1+ - the padding fixed in the after-seed-41 batch).

The report as the run wrote it follows.

# Evolved VM designs

18213 designs evaluated, 17306 alive. Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
